## Exact coverage and visual resolution specification

### Exclusive embedded Buy ownership handoff

Actual L5 admission after K rejected the Buy owner: the raw primary claim has
96 owners; the historical Cursor Redmi claim has 69 and still includes Buy.
The independently reviewed K-bound preparation provision admits metadata4 only.
Introduce sole-parent, metadata-only M after exact K; M apps must equal K.
Original policy bytes and every original claim remain history, not edited policy.
After independently verifying committed M, derive a separate root-bound inventory
with only Buy moved from Cursor to primary (97/2/68). Run ordinary uniqueness and
requested-owner membership checks on that inventory, never a membership exception.
K-only/working/index drafts cannot activate transfer. Other roots remain unchanged.
Recheck the bounded handoff/writer facts before mutation; the cooperative locks
and clean handed-off worktree do not prove universal external-process inactivity.

them. External file placement alone is not technical protection from an agent
with filesystem access. Neither the capture producer nor its output can approve

### Executable current successor and qualification specification

This prospective specification binds metadata16, test-only layout17 and
documentary Q18. It records no future commit SHA and no successful qualification.
The existing 84-case/65-slot contract and all original clauses remain unchanged.
H27 two-tap reachability stays a frontend obligation; it cannot be hidden by a
public-provider deferral. H40 fixture/render/provenance obligations remain
frontend-owned even while authoritative public projection is unavailable.
Two complete source-bound cycles and selected-risk image inspection are required;
an old screenshot count, a preparation pass or a comparison against the current
candidate itself proves none of them. Source15 runtime remains frozen.

#### Current obligation wire contract (prospective metadata16)

Each obligation has exactly `ordinal`, `historicalKey`, `clauses`; clauses retain
the base case order and complete text. Clause and part fields remain those in the
successor specification. `asserted` requires nonempty designated `successfulTests`
and equally many `assertionEvidence` entries, paired by index. Each excerpt must
belong to the paired successful test and explain how its assertion proves this
specific requirement; source/hash verification alone does not prove that meaning.
Both cycles must contain the exact successful report/execution/test tuple.
`asserted` has null presentation authority and dependency. `superseded_presentation`
has the same current assertions and successes plus exactly `path`, `sha256`,
`decision` in presentation authority; its specific case interpretation must match
an independently pinned permitted founder record, not a Q-supplied approval.
It has null dependency. `provider_unmet` has empty success/assertion arrays, null
presentation authority and exactly `owner`, `completion` matching its retained
H27 or H34 deferral. Only the three literal permitted tuples may use it.
H27 clause4 retains both specified parts; H40 frontend clauses are never waived.
Retention/success joins, source-assertion semantics, founder-authority authentication
and inspected visuals remain independent mandatory qualification conjuncts.

Skipped tests outside H84 are not exempted generically. The12 exact existing
`COUNTERRELAUNCH review Store recovery` mode-dependent test keys enumerated in
`Read-OctoberCompatibilityCurrentSkippedCoverage` use complementary mappings:
the11 enabled-fixture keys skip only in impacted_store_buy/default and must succeed
in enabled_review_fixture/default in both cycles; the disabled-review key skips
only in enabled_review_fixture/default and must succeed in impacted_store_buy/default
in both cycles. Atomic recovery source is unchanged by the layout-only successor.
Unknown keys, wrong modes/executions and success elsewhere remain failures.

Assertion excerpt hashes use strict UTF8 source bytes from the committed Git blob,
CRLF normalized to LF, selected inclusive one-based lines joined by LF without an
added trailing newline. The source blob must resolve at captureSource17 and its
Git object identity is recomputed from raw bytes, independently of host encoding.
Source-file/excerpt verification does not authenticate named-test body association
or clause meaning; both must be reviewed against the paired successful test before
qualification. A nonempty reviewObservation or a matching hash alone is not a pass.

Excerpt normalization preserves spaces, blank lines, an initial UTF8 BOM and lone
CR characters as source content; only CRLF becomes LF. A terminal split artifact
is not a selectable line. The captured source must resolve to a commit (not a tree
or blob), and Git overrides are rejected before source-resolution reads.

Current producer intermediate (11 October, not qualified): behavior-only execution
is wired to the current shared planner, schema2 envelope and retained-file reader.
It independently requires clean sealed Stage18/Source17 before side effects,
reconstructs the canonical complete plan before launching, reserves three outputs
with CreateNew, restores Flutter support before postflight, closes output handles
before hashing and preserves failed files. Selected-risk mode explicitly refuses
before source/file/native operations; it is not silently downgraded. The old
unreachable schema1 capture body was removed, not historical validators/fixtures.

This intermediate is not ready to commit or use for qualification. Native stdout
and stderr are copied separately from the fixed batch bridge into reserved streams.
Fourteen actual host transport controls pass on both PowerShell versions: spaced
arguments,4096 simultaneous lines per stream, nonzero exit with retained output,
zero exit with stderr, unsupported arguments and failing destination streams.
They use a labelled standalone batch fixture, not Flutter application journeys.
Parent-exited/open-pipe timeout coverage remains pending. The termination helper's
five-second stop-wait result is now checked; a false result is retained as failure,
not treated as exit proof. Eleven labelled full-launcher orchestration controls
pass on both PowerShell versions, covering successful output closure, collisions,
restoration failure, missing/drifted/throwing postflight, native failure and
malformed retained-output rejection. They mock the source/planner/support/native
and final reader boundaries and do not establish genuine source admission or
retained-reader qualification. The actual double-disposal defect found by these
controls is fixed by nulling successfully closed resource slots before final
cleanup. Independent fixture expectations, exact marker/source snapshots and
generated-root reparse checks were reviewed by the architect. Thirteen labelled preflight refusal controls
exercise the actual launcher function with mocked source/planner boundaries; they
are not successful native executions. Source15 cannot produce current captures.
The full selected-risk binding, Source17 caller suppression, current Q4/Stage19
qualification and both behavioral cycles remain mandatory before metadata16 or any
qualified successor is published. APK and provider obligations remain unchanged.

Selected transport prerequisite (11 October, not capture admission): the native
bridge supports only the converter's exact compact selected-slot JSON spelling,
with sorted unique identifiers and a strict quote round-trip. The only permitted
exclamation is the canonical exact-name selector's negative-lookahead suffix;
ordinary expansion arguments remain rejected and delayed expansion is disabled.
Both PowerShell versions execute a labelled Flutter fixture with the JSON define
and regex together: exactly two intended tests and the single hidden loader
succeed, every other test is rejected, and ten malformed-selector controls fail
before process creation. Existing fourteen separate-stream transport controls
also pass on both hosts. The PS5 array nesting difference was corrected by direct
JSON assignment, not by relaxing canonical checks. Source/risk/test association,
selected-artifact observations and the actual selected capture launcher remain
separate mandatory work; this transport result cannot establish qualification.

Selected-slot literal binding (11 October, not risk review): the shared planner
now derives each permitted slot's original case and exact layout-test key from
the base contract. Selected test names must equal that derived set, not merely
be arbitrary names available in the same profile. Multiple slots from one case
require one test; wrong, extra, missing or foreign-owner bindings are rejected.
Eleven labelled binding controls pass on both hosts, including exact returned
names, one exact regex selector and canonical selected-slot defines. Existing
39 plan,40 receipt and34 event-protocol controls pass on both hosts with correct
case metadata in their labelled contract fixtures. This checks literal identity,
not actual callback execution, clause meaning, geometry or visual equivalence.

Current geometry and inventory boundary (11 October, structural only): geometry
requires exact nested viewport, inset and boundary fields with finite numbers,
independent DPR/raster ratio, positive text scale and integer raster dimensions
equal to ceil(boundary size times raster ratio). Normalized viewport/insets are
logical units and boundary coordinates are local paint units; observation callers
must derive these from actual recorder fields, converting raw FlutterView physical
insets by DPR rather than treating raw pixels as logical units. No label or fixture
can substitute for those observations. Twenty-seven labelled geometry controls
pass on both hosts with singleton-boolean output required.

The structural risk selector validates literal representative slot/test/profile
bindings and returns only representatives in the requested profile. Twenty-five
labelled controls pass on both hosts, including exact selected records, reversed
input/mixed-profile selection and covered nonrepresentatives not being captured
automatically. Equivalence entries remain unverified beyond their structural
count/object checks; no rationale text establishes equivalence or approval.

A strict SHA-bound file adapter accepts only the fixed build-root inventory
filename, an exact schema1/source-role/inventory wrapper and independently derived
current capture-source roles. It holds a FileShare.Read lease, checks the opened
handle's 4MiB limit before hashing/allocation, parses verified bytes with duplicate-key
rejection and rechecks the original file hash and source-state identity before returning.
The generic lease helper's existing64MiB default remains unchanged.
Fifteen real-file controls with labelled source/contract mocks pass on PS7 and PS5:
valid selection, hash/schema/wrapper/key/role/path/profile refusal, source refusal,
post-read source/state drift, denied-write failure/cleanup, writer exclusion and
lease release, growth-before-lease rejection before hashing/allocation, and a
separately labelled injected final-hash mismatch with reached-call proof. A denied
write is not proof of rejecting actually changed file bytes. This is not genuine clean
Source17 admission. Producer context, post-execution/aggregate fences, actual
geometry/artifacts/equivalence inspection, Q composition and index-based current
qualification remain pending. The selected producer remains closed. No new capture,
publication, integration, provider or APK authority is created by these helpers.

Selection-context construction (11 October, not execution binding): the factory

or accepting those tests. The additional reversed-multiple positive verifies sorted

Retained selection binding (11 October, host fixtures only): shared strict inventory

Stage18 launch guard. The retained sidecar reader holds bounded read leases for

Forty-five labelled protocol cases and20 real-file sidecar plus9 composite bundle

Selected producer wiring (11 October, activation still closed): the internal

Measured recorder geometry conjunct: consume the byte-reader result and require

separately. Capture raster ratio is independent of DPR; decoded dimensions must

Recompute the global boundary using the recorded column-major matrix and all

inside/outside roundoff versus exact reviewed geometry. These extend the prior73

composition, source-body/clause semantics, equivalence, visual inspection and

Review trust constraint: structured reviewObservation can bind exact test/body,
assertion values and loop instance to committed source, but cannot itself prove
that review occurred. Actual primary review and image inspection must be retained
independently of candidate Q, with exact source/image hashes and independently

prose alone is not authority. The metadata-only Source15 preparation wrapper
cannot confer Source17 review/capture authority. Establish the narrowly scoped
trusted review workflow before using its records in the operative conjunction;
do not claim machine authentication of judgment or founder visual approval.

Primary review trust model for the remaining mechanism: procedural attestation
with integrity checks under the already delegated primary technical-review scope,
not cryptographic reviewer identity or tamper isolation. The consumer must select
the expected review binding independently of candidate Q through the existing
primary TASK evidence workflow. Deterministic Source17-derived report/seal paths
alone do not establish authorship; agreement between two candidate-controlled
files is insufficient. Independently record the exact source/report/seal digest
before Q preparation, preserve original evidence on correction and recheck all
originals at the final boundary. Do not silently treat a source-access receipt
as comprehension. Actual clause/body/loop review and exact-image inspection are
primary judgments; mechanical checks bind those judgments to retained evidence.
This workflow and the complete operative conjunction are not implemented yet.

### Source23 stored-order capture correction admission

The fresh Source23 StoreLive run failed because its direct-Ready fixture lacked
a stored order identity; the current runtime correctly refuses that selection.
The delivery-review callback passed and remains unchanged. The Founder callback
also requires post-Mark ready identity/row rebinding, as demonstrated by source;
it has not yet run natively. Preserve the failed Source23 receipt and log.

The minimum successor admits only these two test callbacks, with actual
Preparing/packed/Mark ready promotion and post-promotion invariant snapshots.
Metadata24, layout25 and qualification26 are separate bounded steps. Runtime15,
all Source0-23 seals, original 84 obligations, 65 slots, two fresh full cycles,
actual selected-image inspection and H27/H34/H40 provider deferrals remain.
This admission is not qualification, integration authority or APK acceptance.
